## 步骤

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| adapter | AgentAdapterEngineer | verified | 无 | Implemented opt-in legion_edit_file SDK custom tool for edit-only or combined execution grants; PM conversation stays read-only. ProposalService converts typed edit requests to edit_file ActionProposal with trusted session/run, expected full-file SHA and ordered replacements, omitting content. Existing pending typed write responses and cancellation remain. Configured unit passed 50/50 with TMPDIR=/private/tmp and localhost permission; no self-verification requested. |
| core-package | ProjectManager | verified | adapter | Runtime 0.6.0 local package exports pure file ownership and overlap contracts, full-file read SHA and controlled edit via existing public paths; strict 32-file offline install smoke passes with legacy write/read preserved. Desktop manifest/lock point to actual 0.6 tarball and offline npm ci installed it. Historical 0.4/0.5 tarballs unchanged. Electron binary requires downstream install after npm ci --ignore-scripts. |
| desktop | DesktopEngineer | verified | core-package | Desktop owner-map migration, cross-role and repeated-file plan validation, explicit transfer/digest confirmation, atomic owner+plan persistence, owner-bound task grants and edit-file execution, UI owner/transfer/result display, and focused tests/docs completed. Assigned unit check passed 31/31 with unchanged candidate; no self-verification. |
| desktop-package | ProjectManager | verified | desktop | Desktop dependency verifier and strict package checks now bind runtime 0.6.0, 41 workbench exports, 32-file/30-source identity, owner map, explicit transfer and controlled edit. Windows ZIP/native chooser/DPAPI assertions remain; macOS installed-package branch passed and expressly records Windows ZIP not executed. PM user/architecture/distribution/changelog documents updated. Configured desktop-package check passed on unchanged candidate; no manifest or lock edit. |
| reviewer | Reviewer | blocked | desktop-package | Reproduced persistent owner CAS race: two ProjectServiceImpl instances sharing one userData both read owners-v0, both save conflicting Backend/Frontend claims with the same expected digest, and both return success; last provider rename overwrites first. Added deterministic Reviewer regression requiring exactly one success, stale_file_owners loser, and persisted winner. Configured review check captured 31 pass/8 fail/1 Windows ZIP skip on this candidate; one genuine CAS failure, seven stale Reviewer 0.5/34-export/three-tool fixture expectations against 0.6/four-tool product. Desktop owner repair and fresh Reviewer rerun required. |
| tester | Tester | pending | reviewer |  |
